# Master Operational Calendar — Oct 2026 → Jun 2030
## Calgary → Beijing · CS (Humanoid Robotics & AI) · Violin · Jiu-Jitsu · Swimming · Yoga

**Generated:** deterministic build (`calendar/gen.py`). **Weeks:** 197
(Mon 2026-09-21 → Sun 2030-06-30).

> **How to read this.** Every week opens with a **Critical Path — Meetings &
> Applications** lane: the application or meeting *due* that week, the enabling
> action to *start now* (with lead time), and a forward look-ahead — so nothing
> arrives as a surprise. Each week contains what the *next* weeks depend on.
>
> **Pacing.** This is deliberately slow and sequential: one major new commitment
> per term, growth over intensity, rest protected. Phase 1 (now → Dec 2026) is an
> application + preparation season — **there is no coursework until January 2027.**
>
> **Honesty.** Term dates use real published UCalgary values where available and are
> labelled *tentative* or *provisional* where not. Add/drop, withdrawal, exam and
> tuition dates that UCalgary has not yet published are marked as such — never invented.
> The old "10-course Open Studies threshold" is **not** a real rule; the real gate is the
> **Undergraduate Admission Guarantee** (18 Open Studies units + GPA ≥2.50 Science +
> English 30-1). There is no official UCalgary CHIN→HSK mapping; HSK levels are honest
> estimates. Confirm anything money- or immigration-critical at the source.

**Phase map**

| Phase | Window | Where | What |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2026-09-21 → 2026-12-31 | 100 Howse Terrace, Calgary | Pre-Launch (applications + prep) |
| 2 | 2027-01-01 → 2027-04-30 | Calgary | Winter 2027 Open Studies |

**Standard week shape** — Violin 45 min daily · BJJ 3× (Mon/Wed/Fri) · Swim 3×
(Tue/Thu/Sat) · Yoga 3× (Sun/Tue/Thu, active recovery) · LeetCode in daily blocks ·
one long technical block on the weekend. Each week carries an explicit hour budget
and **protected personal time**.

**Discipline growth path** (slow, staged, no rush)

| Phase | Violin | BJJ | Swim | Yoga |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Calgary | Foundation (Suzuki 1-2) | White belt fundamentals | Aerobic base | Hip + shoulder opening |
| 2-3 Calgary | Shifting, vibrato, ensemble-ready | White → blue | Threshold / IM | Mobility + balance |
| 4-5 Calgary | Ensemble + first audition prep | Blue depth, comp rounds | Test sets | Power + restorative |
| 6-8 China | Chamber ensemble (trimmed) | International academy | Olympic-pool sets | Studio flow |
| 9 Calgary | Orchestra-ready, audition rep | Blue → purple | Peak / time trials | Hot yoga + restorative |

**Prerequisite chain** — what must come before what (the plan enforces this order)

| Track | Sequence |
|---|---|
| Programming | C++ & Python fundamentals -> ROS2 nodes -> Isaac Sim -> Project 1 (VLM->kinematics) -> Project 2 (LLM agent) -> Capstone |
| Algorithms | Blind 75 -> NeetCode 150 (pass 1) -> timed pass 2 -> contests -> timed pass 3 -> interviews |
| Chinese | Prep -> CHIN 205 -> 207 -> 301 -> 303 (~HSK 4) -> exchange -> HSK 5 |
| Academic | Open Studies -> Admission Guarantee -> BSc CS major -> co-op -> exchange -> final year -> capstone |
| Money | Income Support (to Dec 2026) -> Student Aid full-time stream (Jan 2027+) via >=9 units / approved co-op |

---

**Each phase ends on a GATE, not a date.** The gate criteria (printed in every
week under *Gate & Feedback*) are the prerequisites for the next phase. If a gate
is not met, repeat or repair before advancing — the plan is sequential, not
calendrical.

> - Check the interim/temporary document: confirm it covers you until the card arrives, and note its validity.
> - Pay and keep the receipt (file it with your records).
